//===== rAthena Script =======================================
//= Nameless Island Quests
//===== Description: =========================================
//= [Official Conversion]
//= Collection of Nameless Island Quests
//= Nameless Island:
//= - Quest to gain access to Nameless Island and dungeon.
//= - Variable in use: aru_monas  (Max: 26)
//= Peace for Arunafeltz:
//- - Help to keep peace between Arunafeltz and Midgard.
//= - Variable in use: aru_em (Max: 23)
//= Muff's Loan:
//- - Help Muff clear his Debt and get his collateral back.
//= - prerequisite Quest to "Broken Diamond"
//= - Variable in use: diamond_edq (Max: 14)
//= Broken Diamond:
//- - Help Ibrahim retrieve his stolen diamond.
//= - prerequisite Quest to "Unlucky Emerald"
//= - Variable in use: diamond_edq (Max: 26)
//= Z-Gang Quest:
//- - Help to aprehend the infamous Z-Gang.
//= - Variable in use: zdan_edq (Max: 19)
//= Unlucky Emerald:
//- - Help a treasure hunter from Prontera get home, and 
//=   learn the origins of the Unlucky Emerald.
//= - Variable in use: treasure_nd (Max: 12)
//= - Variable in use: jewel_nd (Max: 31)
//= Rogue Guild Investigation Assistance:
//- - Assist the Rogue Guild with their investigations.
//= - Variable in use: rumour_nd (Max: 22)

airplane_01,90,63,0	script	事件开关#pc	-1,3,3,{
	end;

OnTouch:
	if ((prt_curse == 36 || prt_curse == 45 || prt_curse == 56 || prt_curse == 61) && (aru_monas < 1)) {
		hideoffnpc "密探#pc1";
		mes "[????]";
		mes "你是"+strcharinfo(0)+"吗？";
		mes "我一直在等你。";
		mes "^6B8E23巴姆夫神父^000000在恭候";
		mes "您前往^6B8E23普隆德拉";
		mes "教堂^000000。请尽快";
		mes "去拜访他。";
		set aru_monas,1;
		//setquest 17000;
		close2;
		hideonnpc "密探#pc1";
	}
	end;
}

cmd_in02,174,89,7	script	普通男子#pc1	97,{
	if (aru_monas < 2) {
		mes "[拉尔杰斯]";
		mes "可恶，我又输了！";
		mes "这游戏肯定有诈！";
		mes "不管我怎么试，";
		mes "就是赢不了！";
		mes "可是克魔岛德州扑克";
		mes "实在太……太让人上瘾了！";
		close;
	}
	else if (aru_monas == 2) {
		mes "[拉尔杰斯]";
		mes "唉，这游戏我输了";
		mes "太多次，已经";
		mes "完全记不清了！";
		mes "我总得赢一次吧……";
		next;
		select("打扰一下。");
		mes "[拉尔杰斯]";
		mes "哦！呃……嗯……";
		mes "有什么可以为您效劳的？";
		next;
		select ("^6B8E23巴姆夫神父^000000派我来的。");
		mes "[拉尔杰斯]";
		mes "哦，原来如此。抱歉，";
		mes "如果我大吵大闹";
		mes "让你有点不自在了。";
		mes "我只是来打发";
		mes "时间的……现在我亲眼";
		mes "见识到赌博有多邪恶了！";
		next;
		mes "[拉尔杰斯]";
		mes "呃，你能过来";
		mes "这边一下吗？我不";
		mes "想让别人";
		mes "听到我们的谈话。";
		close2;
		set aru_monas,3;
		warp "cmd_in02",110,53;
		end;
	}
	else {
		mes "[拉尔杰斯]";
		mes "什么？！我又输了？";
		mes "这游戏到底……到底";
		mes "是怎么玩的？！";
		close;
	}
}

ra_in01,308,59,1	script	服务员#pc	930,{
	if (aru_monas < 7) {
		mes "[侍者]";
		mes "欢迎来到";
		mes "拉赫之味，是拉赫";
		mes "最上等、最精致的";
		mes "餐厅之一。";
		if (aru_monas < 6) {
			close;
		}
		if (aru_monas == 6) {
			next;
			mes "[侍者]";
			mes "您想点些";
			mes "什么？还是说……如果我没";
			mes "猜错，您来这儿";
			mes "是为了别的事？";
			next;
			if (select("我想点餐。:是的，我是为别的事而来。") == 1) {
				mes "[侍者]";
				mes "当然，没问题。";
				mes "您一个人来的吗？";
				next;
				select("Yes.");
				mes "[侍者]";
				mes "这边请。";
				close2;
				set aru_monas,7;
				switch(rand(1,4)) {
					case 1: warp "ra_in01",303,43; end;
					case 2: warp "ra_in01",304,43; end;
					case 3: warp "ra_in01",304,39; end;
					case 4: warp "ra_in01",303,39; end;
				}
			}
			mes "[侍者]";
			mes "嗯，好吧。不管";
			mes "是什么事，希望您在";
			mes "拉赫之味过得愉快。";
			close;
		}
	}
	mes "[侍者]";
	mes "这家餐厅的";
	mes "料理是拉赫";
	mes "最好的——不，";
	mes "是整个阿鲁纳贝兹";
	mes "最棒的！";
	close;
}
